#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The current kernel is the cache-unfriendly `i-j-k` form and has no OpenMP linkage. I’m using row ownership plus cache blocking so threads write disjoint output rows, while the innermost loop stays contiguous and vectorizable; this avoids synchronization in the hot path and preserves ascending-`k` accumulation for each result.
  ✓ Inspect source, build configuration, and benchmark contract
  → Implement performance-oriented OpenMP matrix multiplication
  • Build, test correctness, and tune compiler/runtime details

 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // One thread owns each output tile, so the hot loop needs no atomics or
+    // reductions.  The tile sizes keep C and the current panels of A and B in
+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 16;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t blockDepth = 64;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    c[i * N + j] = 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+
+            for (size_t depthBlock = 0; depthBlock < N; depthBlock += blockDepth) {
+                const size_t depthEnd = std::min(depthBlock + blockDepth, N);
+
+                for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                    double* const cRow = c + i * N;
+                    const double* const aRow = a + i * N;
+
+                    for (size_t k = depthBlock; k < depthEnd; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = aRow[k];
+                        const double* const bRow = b + k * N;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                            cRow[j] += aValue * bRow[j];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 16;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t blockDepth = 64;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    c[i * N + j] = 0.0;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+
+            for (size_t depthBlock = 0; depthBlock < N; depthBlock += blockDepth) {
+                const size_t depthEnd = std::min(depthBlock + blockDepth, N);
+
+                for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                    double* const cRow = c + i * N;
+                    const double* const aRow = a + i * N;
+
+                    for (size_t k = depthBlock; k < depthEnd; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = aRow[k];
+                        const double* const bRow = b + k * N;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                            cRow[j] += aValue * bRow[j];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

codex
The first implementation builds with required OpenMP linkage and passes validation at boundary sizes around every tile dimension. I’m now checking scaling and cache-tile behavior across thread counts; the main risk at this point is oversubscription/topology overhead, not correctness.

 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // One thread owns each output tile, so the hot loop needs no atomics or
+    // reductions.  The tile sizes keep C and the current panels of A and B in
+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 16;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 16;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            // A 4x16 register micro-kernel reuses each loaded B vector for
+            // four rows.  Keeping the partial sums local avoids loading and
+            // storing C for every value of k.
+            for (; i + 3 < rowEnd; i += 4) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sum0[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum1[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum2[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum3[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+                        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+                        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+                        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+                            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+                            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+                            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+                            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+                        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+                        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+                        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // Handle a partial row tile without padding or special matrix
+            // allocation, retaining support for every matrix size.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sums[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+            // A 4x16 register micro-kernel reuses each loaded B vector for
+            // four rows.  Keeping the partial sums local avoids loading and
+            // storing C for every value of k.
+            for (; i + 3 < rowEnd; i += 4) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sum0[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum1[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum2[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum3[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+                        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+                        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+                        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+                            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+                            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+                            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+                            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+                        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+                        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+                        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // Handle a partial row tile without padding or special matrix
+            // allocation, retaining support for every matrix size.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sums[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // One thread owns each output tile, so the hot loop needs no atomics or
+    // reductions.  The tile sizes keep C and the current panels of A and B in
+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 16;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 4;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            // An 8x4 register micro-kernel reuses each loaded B vector for
+            // eight rows. Keeping the partial sums local avoids loading and
+            // storing C for every value of k.
+            for (; i + 7 < rowEnd; i += 8) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sum0[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum1[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum2[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum3[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum4[microCols] = {};

+                    alignas(64) double sum6[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum7[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+                        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+                        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+                        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+                        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+                        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+                        const double a6 = a[(i + 6) * N + k];

+            // allocation, retaining support for every matrix size.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sums[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // One thread owns each output tile, so the hot loop needs no atomics or
+    // reductions.  The tile sizes keep C and the current panels of A and B in
+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            // A 6x8 register micro-kernel reuses each loaded B vector for six
+            // rows. Keeping the partial sums local avoids loading and
+            // storing C for every value of k.
+            for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sum0[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum1[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum2[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum3[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum4[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum5[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+                        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+                        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+                        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+                        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+                        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+                            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+                            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+                            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+                            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+                            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+                            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;

+                        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+                        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];

+                        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+                    }
+                }

 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // One thread owns each output tile, so the hot loop needs no atomics or
+    // reductions.  The tile sizes keep C and the current panels of A and B in
+    // cache, while j is contiguous and can use the full SIMD width.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            // A 6x8 register micro-kernel reuses each loaded B vector for six
+            // rows. Keeping the partial sums local avoids loading and
+            // storing C for every value of k.
+            for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sum0[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum1[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum2[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum3[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum4[microCols] = {};
+                    alignas(64) double sum5[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+                        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+                        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+                        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+                        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+                        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+                            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+                            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+                            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+                            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+                            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+                            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+                        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+                        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+                        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+                        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+                        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+
+            // Handle a partial row tile without padding or special matrix
+            // allocation, retaining support for every matrix size.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sums[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

+                        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+                        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+                        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+                            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+                            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+                            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+                            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+                            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+                            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+                        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+                        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+                        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+                        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+                        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+
+            // Handle a partial row tile without padding or special matrix
+            // allocation, retaining support for every matrix size.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                for (size_t j = colBlock; j < colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, colEnd - j);
+                    alignas(64) double sums[microCols] = {};
+
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+                        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+                        }
+                    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
 }

codex
Compiler diagnostics confirmed SIMD generation, but also exposed avoidable stack copies from variable-width micro-tiles. I’m specializing the full-tile hot path so partial sums remain in vector registers; odd-size edge handling stays separate and correctness-preserving.
apply patch

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
+// Fixed-width micro-kernels let the compiler keep all partial sums in SIMD
+// registers throughout the k loop. They are inlined into each OpenMP tile.
+inline void multiply4x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+    }
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 16;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            for (; i + 3 < rowEnd; i += 4) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    multiply4x8(a, b, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum0 = 0.0;
+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double bValue = b[k * N + j];
+                        sum0 += a[(i + 0) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum1 += a[(i + 1) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum2 += a[(i + 2) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                    }
+                    c[(i + 0) * N + j] = sum0;
+                    c[(i + 1) * N + j] = sum1;
+                    c[(i + 2) * N + j] = sum2;
+                    c[(i + 3) * N + j] = sum3;
+                }

+
+            // Handle a final partial group of rows for arbitrary N.

+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    multiply1x8(a, b, c, N, i, j);
+                }

+                        sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                    }
+                    c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                }
+            }
         }
     }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;

+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+inline void multiply6x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+

+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];

+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];

+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];

+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd

+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};

+        }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+        for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    multiply6x8(a, b, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum0 = 0.0;
+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    double sum4 = 0.0;
+                    double sum5 = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double bValue = b[k * N + j];
+                        sum0 += a[(i + 0) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum1 += a[(i + 1) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum2 += a[(i + 2) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum4 += a[(i + 4) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum5 += a[(i + 5) * N + k] * bValue;
+                    }
+                    c[(i + 0) * N + j] = sum0;
+                    c[(i + 1) * N + j] = sum1;
+                    c[(i + 2) * N + j] = sum2;
+                    c[(i + 3) * N + j] = sum3;
+                    c[(i + 4) * N + j] = sum4;
+                    c[(i + 5) * N + j] = sum5;
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+
+            // Handle a final partial group of rows for arbitrary N.
+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    multiply1x8(a, b, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                    }
+                    c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                }
+            }
         }
     }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }

+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)

+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+            size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+            for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;

+                    const double* const bPanel =

+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {

+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    double sum4 = 0.0;
+                    double sum5 = 0.0;

+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum4 += a[(i + 4) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum5 += a[(i + 5) * N + k] * bValue;

+                    c[(i + 4) * N + j] = sum4;
+                    c[(i + 5) * N + j] = sum5;
+                }
+            }

+            // Handle a final partial group of rows for arbitrary N.

+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }

+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+            const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+            const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+            size_t i = rowBlock;

+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }

+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum0 = 0.0;
+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    double sum4 = 0.0;
+                    double sum5 = 0.0;

+                        const double bValue = b[k * N + j];
+                        sum0 += a[(i + 0) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum1 += a[(i + 1) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum2 += a[(i + 2) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum4 += a[(i + 4) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum5 += a[(i + 5) * N + k] * bValue;
+                    }
+                    c[(i + 0) * N + j] = sum0;
+                    c[(i + 1) * N + j] = sum1;
+                    c[(i + 2) * N + j] = sum2;
+                    c[(i + 3) * N + j] = sum3;
+                    c[(i + 4) * N + j] = sum4;
+                    c[(i + 5) * N + j] = sum5;
+                }
+            }

+            for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                    }
+                    c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        }
     }
 }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * N + j;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+                for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum0 = 0.0;
+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    double sum4 = 0.0;
+                    double sum5 = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {

+                        sum1 += a[(i + 1) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum2 += a[(i + 2) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum4 += a[(i + 4) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum5 += a[(i + 5) * N + k] * bValue;
+                    }
+                    c[(i + 0) * N + j] = sum0;
+                    c[(i + 1) * N + j] = sum1;
+                    c[(i + 2) * N + j] = sum2;
+                    c[(i + 3) * N + j] = sum3;
+                    c[(i + 4) * N + j] = sum4;
+                    c[(i + 5) * N + j] = sum5;
+                }
+            }
+
+                // Handle a final partial group of rows for arbitrary N.
+                for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {

+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                    }
+                    c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                }
+                }
+            }
+        }
     }
 }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {

+// This fixed-width micro-kernel keeps all partial sums in SIMD registers and
+// reuses every B vector across six output rows.
+inline void multiply6x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum40 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum41 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum50 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum51 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+        const double* const bValues = b + k * 8;
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues + 4);
+

+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);

+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 2) * N + k);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 3) * N + k);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+        const __m256d a4 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 4) * N + k);
+        sum40 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b0, sum40);
+        sum41 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b1, sum41);

+        sum50 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b0, sum50);
+        sum51 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b1, sum51);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 0) * N + j, sum00);

+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j + 4, sum21);

+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j + 4, sum51);

+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd

+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t i = rowBlock;
+
+                for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum0 = 0.0;
+                    double sum1 = 0.0;
+                    double sum2 = 0.0;
+                    double sum3 = 0.0;
+                    double sum4 = 0.0;
+                    double sum5 = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        const double bValue = b[k * N + j];
+                        sum0 += a[(i + 0) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum1 += a[(i + 1) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum2 += a[(i + 2) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum3 += a[(i + 3) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum4 += a[(i + 4) * N + k] * bValue;
+                        sum5 += a[(i + 5) * N + k] * bValue;
+                    }
+                    c[(i + 0) * N + j] = sum0;
+                    c[(i + 1) * N + j] = sum1;
+                    c[(i + 2) * N + j] = sum2;
+                    c[(i + 3) * N + j] = sum3;
+                    c[(i + 4) * N + j] = sum4;
+                    c[(i + 5) * N + j] = sum5;
+                }
+            }
+
+                // Handle a final partial group of rows for arbitrary N.
+                for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                }
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                        sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                    }
+                    c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                }
+                }
+            }
+        }
     }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];

+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.

+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.

+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);

   248	// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
   249	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   250	                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   251	    // Check a few random positions
   252	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   253	    
   254	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   255	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   256	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   257	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   258	            
   259	            double expected = 0.0;
   260	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   261	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   262	            }
   263	            
   264	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   265	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   266	            
   267	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   268	                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   269	                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   270	                return false;
   271	            }
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	    
   275	    return true;
   276	}

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {

+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)

+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t i = rowBlock;

+                    }

+                }

+// This fixed-width micro-kernel keeps all partial sums in SIMD registers and

+inline void multiply6x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+    __m256d sum51 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues + 4);

+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 3) * N + k);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);

+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};

+#else
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 6;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
