+        const __m256d a4 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 4) * N + k);
+        sum40 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b0, sum40);
+        sum41 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b1, sum41);
+        const __m256d a5 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 5) * N + k);
+        sum50 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b0, sum50);
+        sum51 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b1, sum51);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 0) * N + j, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 0) * N + j + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j + 4, sum31);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j, sum40);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j + 4, sum41);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j, sum50);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j + 4, sum51);
+#else
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * width;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 6;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(panelCount * N * microCols);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns the strided B accesses in the original
+        // loop order into sequential streams for the register micro-kernel.
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
             for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+                const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                    destination[x] = source[x];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

codex
The packed layout roughly doubles single-socket throughput, while direct access scales better once many cores collectively hide the strided reads. I’m combining those two cache behaviors behind an automatic thread-count choice; both paths remain OpenMP-only and produce the same matrix, with no user-facing mode or dependency.

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {

+        }
+    }

+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];

+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;

+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.

+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 32;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 48;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();

+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {

+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+

+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {

+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;

+                    }

+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }

     }

+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 48;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+    __m256d sum51 = _mm256_setzero_pd();

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}
+
+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum40 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum41 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum50 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum51 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues + 4);
+
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 0) * N + k);
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 1) * N + k);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 2) * N + k);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 3) * N + k);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+        const __m256d a4 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 4) * N + k);
+        sum40 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b0, sum40);
+        sum41 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b1, sum41);
+        const __m256d a5 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 5) * N + k);
+        sum50 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b0, sum50);
+        sum51 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b1, sum51);
+    }
+
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 0) * N + j, sum00);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 0) * N + j + 4, sum01);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j + 4, sum31);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j, sum40);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j + 4, sum41);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j, sum50);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j + 4, sum51);
+#else
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(close)
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+// This fixed-width micro-kernel keeps all partial sums in SIMD registers and
+// reuses every B vector across six output rows.
+inline void multiply6x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    __m256d sum00 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum01 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum10 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum11 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum20 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum21 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum30 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum31 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum40 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum41 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum50 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum51 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+        const __m256d b0 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues);
+        const __m256d b1 = _mm256_loadu_pd(bValues + 4);
+
+        const __m256d a0 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 0) * N + k);
+        sum00 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b0, sum00);
+        sum01 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a0, b1, sum01);
+        const __m256d a1 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 1) * N + k);
+        sum10 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b0, sum10);
+        sum11 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a1, b1, sum11);
+        const __m256d a2 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 2) * N + k);
+        sum20 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b0, sum20);
+        sum21 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a2, b1, sum21);
+        const __m256d a3 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 3) * N + k);
+        sum30 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b0, sum30);
+        sum31 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a3, b1, sum31);
+        const __m256d a4 = _mm256_broadcast_sd(a + (i + 4) * N + k);
+        sum40 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a4, b0, sum40);

+        sum51 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(a5, b1, sum51);
+    }

+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j, sum10);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 1) * N + j + 4, sum11);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j, sum20);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 2) * N + j + 4, sum21);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j, sum30);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 3) * N + j + 4, sum31);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j, sum40);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 4) * N + j + 4, sum41);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j, sum50);
+    _mm256_storeu_pd(c + (i + 5) * N + j + 4, sum51);
+#else
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sum0[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum1[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum2[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum3[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};
+    alignas(64) double sum5[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+        const double a0 = a[(i + 0) * N + k];
+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];

+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

   154	void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   155	                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   156	    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
   157	    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
   158	    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
   159	    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
   160	    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
   161	
   162	    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
   163	    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
   164	    double* __restrict c = C.data();
   165	    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
   166	    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
   167	    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
   168	    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }

+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;

+        const double a1 = a[(i + 1) * N + k];
+        const double a2 = a[(i + 2) * N + k];
+        const double a3 = a[(i + 3) * N + k];
+        const double a4 = a[(i + 4) * N + k];
+        const double a5 = a[(i + 5) * N + k];
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            const double bValue = bValues[x];
+            sum0[x] += a0 * bValue;
+            sum1[x] += a1 * bValue;
+            sum2[x] += a2 * bValue;
+            sum3[x] += a3 * bValue;
+            sum4[x] += a4 * bValue;
+            sum5[x] += a5 * bValue;
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[(i + 0) * N + j + x] = sum0[x];
+        c[(i + 1) * N + j + x] = sum1[x];
+        c[(i + 2) * N + j + x] = sum2[x];
+        c[(i + 3) * N + j + x] = sum3[x];
+        c[(i + 4) * N + j + x] = sum4[x];
+        c[(i + 5) * N + j + x] = sum5[x];
+    }
+#endif
+}
+
+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }
+}

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+    alignas(64) double sum4[width] = {};

+inline void multiply1x8(const double* __restrict a, const double* __restrict b,
+                        double* __restrict c, const size_t N,
+                        const size_t bStride, const size_t i, const size_t j) {
+    constexpr size_t width = 8;
+    alignas(64) double sums[width] = {};
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+        const double aValue = a[i * N + k];
+        const double* const bValues = b + k * bStride;
+
+#pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+            sums[x] += aValue * bValues[x];
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+        c[i * N + j + x] = sums[x];
+    }

+void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    // Each thread owns complete output tiles, eliminating synchronization in
+    // the hot path and exposing enough independent work to scale across cores.
+    constexpr size_t blockRows = 18;
+    constexpr size_t blockCols = 64;
+    constexpr size_t microCols = 8;
+
+    const double* __restrict a = A.data();
+    const double* __restrict b = B.data();
+    double* __restrict c = C.data();
+    const size_t panelCount = (N + microCols - 1) / microCols;
+    const bool usePackedB = omp_get_max_threads() <= 40;
+    std::vector<double> packedB(usePackedB ? panelCount * N * microCols : 0);
+    double* __restrict packed = packedB.data();
+
+#pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        // Panel-major storage turns strided reads into sequential streams at
+        // modest thread counts. Large teams use B directly: their aggregate
+        // hardware prefetching is faster than a separate packing pass.
+        if (usePackedB) {
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t panel = 0; panel < panelCount; ++panel) {
+                for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                    const size_t firstCol = panel * microCols;
+                    const size_t width = std::min(microCols, N - firstCol);
+                    const double* const source = b + k * N + firstCol;
+                    double* const destination = packed + (panel * N + k) * microCols;
+#pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
+                        destination[x] = source[x];
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+        for (size_t rowBlock = 0; rowBlock < N; rowBlock += blockRows) {
+            for (size_t colBlock = 0; colBlock < N; colBlock += blockCols) {
+                const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBlock + blockRows, N);
+                const size_t colEnd = std::min(colBlock + blockCols, N);
+                size_t j = colBlock;
+
+                // Reuse one packed B panel across all rows before advancing
+                // to the next panel. A row tile and one B panel fit in L2.
+                for (; j + microCols <= colEnd; j += microCols) {
+                    const double* const bPanel =
+                        usePackedB ? packed + (j / microCols) * N * microCols
+                                   : b + j;
+                    const size_t bStride = usePackedB ? microCols : N;
+                    size_t i = rowBlock;
+                    for (; i + 5 < rowEnd; i += 6) {
+                        multiply6x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        multiply1x8(a, bPanel, c, N, bStride, i, j);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Only non-multiple-of-eight matrix widths use this edge path.
+                for (; j < colEnd; ++j) {
+                    for (size_t i = rowBlock; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[i * N + k] * b[k * N + j];
+                        }
+                        c[i * N + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

Key improvements:

- Parallel initialization and tiled multiplication with static OpenMP scheduling.
- Synchronization-free output-tile ownership.
- Cache-aware packed/direct `B` layouts selected by thread count.
- AVX2/FMA 6×8 register kernel with portable OpenMP SIMD fallback.
- Arbitrary matrix dimensions handled correctly.
- Executable remains `matmul`; no dependencies or files added.

Verification passed across numerous boundary sizes and 1, 8, 41, and 128 threads. On the available host, `N=1536` scaled from 264 ms at one thread to 11 ms at 128 threads.
